Pack Tactics. The dire wolf has Advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of the dire wolf's allies is within 5 feet of the creature and the ally doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

[concept] identity: The dire wolf with a frost-rimed hide circles the pack's quarry in a wide, silent arc, letting the rime on its coat crackle and flare before it lunges to tear at a flank or hamstring and then wheels away again, always keeping a body between its prey and the open ground. It never fights alone or holds a line — it runs the edge of the fight in short, freezing dashes, snapping at whoever turns their back, and it knows the crunch of frost-stiffened limbs under its jaws. Cornered or bloodied, it does not flee outright but grows reckless, spending its cold-rimed hide in close, crushing bites until the pack's numbers tell or it dies in the snow.
[concept] lore: Raised in the high passes where a wolf's coat freezes to its own hide, these beasts are the mountain's warning that the cold is hungry — they harry travelers through the drifts to warm their packs on the fallen.

Traits

Amphibious. The antiphon can breathe air and water.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the antiphon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The antiphon makes two Slam attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Resonant Retort.

Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 15 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Thunder damage.

Cacophonous Bellow (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature in a 60-foot Cone. Failure: 78 (12d12) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Resonant Retort. Strength Saving Throw: DC 24, one creature within 30 feet that is Deafened. Failure: 63 (14d8) Thunder damage. Success: Half damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the antiphon can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Lunge and Crush. The antiphon moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Slam attack.

Tide Surge. Strength Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature within 10 feet of the antiphon. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: The Drowned Choir is a bulk of brine-thick flesh and barnacle-crusted hide that rises from the trench with its mouth already open, and the first thing it does in a fight is sing — a low, crushing note that rolls across the water and inside the skulls of everyone within reach. Every round after, it wades into the nearest ship or swimmer and trades blows with two mass-heavy limbs while the song keeps grinding, thickening and turning lethal as the fight drags on. Under pressure it does not retreat; it sinks a little, opens its mouth wider, and calls more bodies out of the deep, fighting to the last with the patience of something that has drowned a hundred fleets.
[concept] lore: It is the eldest thing in the trench, a leviathan whose song is the tide's own voice, and it wants only to be heard — every fleet that sails too close is invited down until the chorus is complete.

Bog Hound
Medium beast, unaligned
Armor Class

11 (natural armor)

22 (3d8 + 9)

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


12 (+1)

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 12

1 (200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Faultless Tracker. The bog hound knows the direction and distance to any creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, as long as the two of them are on the same plane of existence.
Whelm. As a bonus action, the bog hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the bog hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the bog hound's turns. The bog hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the bog hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the bog hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the bog hound. If the bog hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The bog hound makes two Jaws attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Muck Drag.
Jaws: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Muck Drag: One creature within 30 feet that is grappled by the bog hound must make a DC 13 Strength saving throw, taking 11 (2d10) piercing damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.

Immunities Necrotic, Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)

Traits

Funeral Din. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, any creature that starts its turn in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the warden. Failure: The target has the Deafened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Ethereal Sight. The warden can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane when it is on the Material Plane.

Incorporeal Movement. The warden can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Actions

Multiattack. The warden makes two Withering Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Horrific Visage.

Withering Touch. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 19 (3d10 + 3) Necrotic damage.

Death Knell (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 15-foot Emanation originating from the warden. Failure: 13 (3d8) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Horrific Visage. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 60-foot Cone that can see the warden and isn't an Undead. Failure: 10 (2d6 + 3) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of the warden's next turn. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Horrific Visage for 24 hours.

Possession (Recharge 6). Charisma Saving Throw: DC 13, one Humanoid the warden can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is possessed by the warden; the warden disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The warden now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The warden can't be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The warden's game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target's Speed, as well as the target's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers.
The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points or the warden leaves as a Bonus Action. When the possession ends, the warden appears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours.

Etherealness. The warden casts the Etherealness spell, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability. The warden is visible on the Material Plane while on the Border Ethereal and vice versa, but it can't affect or be affected by anything on the other plane.

[concept] identity: The bell-ringer's ghost does not stalk; it announces. On the first round of a fight it tolls its spectral bell, a Necrotic knell that rolls through the chapel and marks the nearest living soul as 'dying' — the clamor bends toward that target and its allies cannot hear themselves think. Every round after, it drifts through the walls to keep its dying mark in sight, tolling again to drain warmth and years from it, refusing to stop while the bell has a name to ring for. It is patient and ritual-bent — a ghost that flees light and holy ground but fights to the last knell once the toll begins, because in its mind the funeral is already underway.
[concept] lore: A chapel bell-ringer who died at the rope mid-stroke, the ghost returns each night believing the parishioners' deaths have gone unannounced, and tolls for whoever in the dark is nearest to dying.

Traits

Splintering Tar. A creature that touches the siege-timber golem or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 7 (2d6) Piercing damage.

Lightning Absorption. Whenever the siege-timber golem is subjected to Lightning damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of Hit Points equal to the Lightning damage dealt.

Immutable Form. The siege-timber golem can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The siege-timber golem has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The siege-timber golem makes two Timber Fist attacks.

Timber Fist. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 27 (5d8 + 5) Bludgeoning damage.

Siege Shoulder (Recharge 5-6). The siege-timber golem spends 5 feet of movement to jump to a space within 20 feet that contains one or more Large or smaller creatures. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 16, each creature in the destination space. Failure: 27 (6d8) Bludgeoning damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage, and the target is pushed 5 feet straight away.

Adult Red Dragon
Huge dragon, chaotic evil
Armor Class

19 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

256 (19d12 + 133)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


27 (+8)

	

10 (+0)

	

25 (+7)

	

16 (+3)

	

13 (+1)

	

23 (+6)

Skills: 

Perception +13, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6, WIS +7

Damage Immunities: 

fire

Senses: 

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 23

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

17 (18,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Blindside Lash. A creature that touches the dragon or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) fire damage.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Sight Weakness. The dragon has disadvantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks that rely on sight.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Come Hither: Each creature of the dragon's choice within 90 feet that can see or hear the dragon must succeed on a DC 20 Wisdom saving throw or be charmed until the start of the dragon's next turn, and while charmed this way, it must move toward the dragon by the safest available route on its turn and can't take reactions.
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

Scree Slide Thing
Large elemental, unaligned
Armor Class

13 (natural armor)

93 (11d10 + 33)

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

tremorsense 60 ft., passive Perception 13

None

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Grinding Shingle. The slide thing adheres to anything that touches it. A Huge or smaller creature adhered to the slide thing is also grappled by it (escape DC 14). Ability checks made to escape this grapple have disadvantage.
Entomb. As a bonus action, the slide thing entombs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While entombed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the slide thing, and takes 7 (2d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the slide thing's turns. The slide thing can have only one creature entombed at a time. If the slide thing takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the entombed creature, the slide thing must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the slide thing. If the slide thing dies, an entombed creature breaks free prone by using 5 feet of movement.
Rasping Rebuke. As a bonus action, the slide thing targets one creature within 5 feet that hit it with an attack since the end of the slide thing's last turn. The target takes 7 (2d6) bludgeoning damage.

Actions
Multiattack: The slide thing makes two Rubble Slam attacks.
Rubble Slam: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
```

Threshold Execute (Recharge 5-6): One creature the stalker can see within 90 feet that has half of its hit points or fewer must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw. On a failed save, if the target has 32 hit points or fewer, it drops to 0 hit points; otherwise, it takes 32 (5d12) cold damage, or half as much damage on a successful save.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the brass tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Aura of Authority. While in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the brass tyrant, the brass tyrant and its allies have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws, provided the brass tyrant doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The brass tyrant makes two Ingot-Hammer attacks.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the brass tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Stride Forth. The brass tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Ingot-Hammer attack.

Bow or Burn. One ally of the brass tyrant that can see or hear it can immediately use its Reaction to move up to half its Speed or to make one attack.

Rally the Forge. The brass tyrant and each of its allies within 10 feet of it that can see or hear it end the Charmed and Frightened conditions on themselves.

[concept] lore: Forged in the first smelter-city and crowned by the guilds it devoured, the Brass Tyrant still rules its streets of forges, demanding tribute in heat and obedience in iron.

Corrosive Form. A creature that touches the sanctum rot or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 9 (2d8) acid damage.
Spider Climb. The sanctum rot can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.

Actions

Relic Lure: One creature within 30 feet of the sanctum rot must succeed on a DC 15 Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 25 feet straight toward the sanctum rot by the relic's pull and be grappled (escape DC 15).

[concept] identity: Krennik Skullstandard opens by loosing his worg into a full gallop, angling the charge so it carries him past a chosen target and lets his swung blade rake the length of it. Every round after that is a re-pass: the worg wheels, circles, and comes back through the gap he opens, rider leaning out to strike while the beast harries heels and flanks. He is a pressuring skirmisher who trusts momentum over armor — when bloodied he does not disengage so much as break contact, reform, and charge again from a new angle, wearing prey down rather than standing to trade blows.
[concept] lore: Krennik Skullstandard was once a border-outrider of some hill warband, and now he and his worg live by riding down stragglers on open roads, claiming the skull-totem standard at his back marks every mile he has made his own.

Medium Aberration, Neutral Evil

AC 14

Languages None

CR 5 (XP 1,800; PB +3)

Actions

Multiattack. The drownedthing makes two Tentacle attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Deep Chill.

Tentacle. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 12 (2d8 + 3) Bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) Cold damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Deep Chill. Strength Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 30 feet that is Grappled by the drownedthing. Failure: 31 (9d6) Cold damage. Success: Half damage.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. The drownedthing swallows one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While swallowed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the drownedthing, and takes 7 (2d6) Cold damage at the start of each of the drownedthing's turns. The drownedthing can have only one creature swallowed at a time. If the drownedthing takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the swallowed creature, the drownedthing must regurgitate the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the drownedthing. If the drownedthing dies, a swallowed creature exits the body Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Traits

Drag the Fresh Kill. While it has a creature Grappled or caged, At the start of each of the klahk's turns, it moves the freshest kill it holds up to its Speed toward its lair, dragging a Grappled creature with it.

Luring Click. The klahk knows the direction and distance to any creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, as long as the two of them are on the same plane of existence.

Actions

Multiattack. The klahk makes two Rib Lattice attacks. It can replace one attack with.

Rib Lattice. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Wind Carry. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 30 feet that has the Grappled condition. Failure: 28 (8d6) Necrotic damage. Success: Half damage.

Bonus Actions

Cage. The klahk cages one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While caged, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the klahk, and takes 6 (1d12) Necrotic damage at the start of each of the klahk's turns. The klahk can have only one creature caged at a time. If the klahk takes 12 damage or more in a single turn from the caged creature, the klahk must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the klahk. If the klahk dies, a caged creature pulls free Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Scheming and patient, it hoards trophies by value, not hunger, and when bloodied it feigns a limp to lure the pursuit into a deadfall narrows. It is scheming (INT 14-16), and it is a hoarder that drags its kills back to a lair of trophies.

Traits

Stolen Voices. The air elemental can mimic animal sounds and humanoid voices. A creature that hears the sounds can tell they are imitations only with a successful DC 13 Wisdom (Insight) check.

Air Form. The air elemental can enter a creature's space and stop there. It can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so.

Actions

Multiattack. The air elemental makes two Thunderous Slam attacks.

Thunderous Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 14 (2d8 + 5) Thunder damage.

Chorus Barrage (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 15-foot Emanation originating from the air elemental. Failure: 18 (4d8) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Whirlwind (Recharge 4-6). Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one Medium or smaller creature in the air elemental's space. Failure: 24 (4d10 + 2) Thunder damage, and the target is pushed up to 20 feet straight away from the air elemental and has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Hydra
Huge monstrosity, unaligned
Armor Class

15 (natural armor)

184 (16d12 + 80)

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

20 (+5)

+3

Smoldering Stumps. The first time the hydra has half of its hit points or fewer in a fight, it releases a burst. Each creature within 15 feet of the hydra must make a DC 16 Constitution saving throw, taking 24 (7d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also blinded until the end of its next turn.
Hold Breath. The hydra can hold its breath for 1 hour.
Multiple Heads. The hydra has five heads. Whenever the hydra takes 25 damage or more on a single turn, one of its heads dies. The hydra dies if all its heads are dead.
Reactive Heads. For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra reaction that can be used only for opportunity attacks.
Smoking Stumps (1/Day). As a bonus action, the hydra creates a scalding, choking smoke in a 20-foot-radius sphere centered on itself. The effect lasts while the hydra maintains concentration on it, up to 10 minutes. Each creature in the area has disadvantage on attack rolls.

Actions
Multiattack: The hydra makes as many Bite attacks as it has heads.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (1d10 + 5) piercing damage.
```

[concept] identity: The smoking hydra is a regenerator gone wrong: it charges in behind a wall of biting heads, and every wound it suffers seals over with a hiss of hot vapor rather than muscle and bone. Each round it lunges and lashes with its remaining necks while fresh stumps pour scalding smoke, building a blinding, choking shroud that hides its true head count and punishes anything that stays close. It never breaks off — a wounded hydra simply makes more smoke, fighting to the death and leaving its prey to guess which necks are real.
[concept] lore: Sparked in the ash-choked calderas where an old hydra was burned down to its roots, this beast regrew wrong — the flesh knits, but the severed necks seal into smoking vents that it now hunts to fuel.

CR 10 (XP 5,900; PB +4)

Traits

Sorrowful Wrath. While Bloodied, the stone golem has Advantage on attack rolls.

Immutable Form. The stone golem can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The stone golem has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The stone golem makes two attacks, using Slam or Force Bolt in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Sorrowful Pilgrimage.

Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 15 (2d8 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 9 (2d8) Force damage.

Force Bolt. Ranged Attack Roll: +9, range 120 ft. 22 (4d10) Force damage.

Sorrowful Pilgrimage. Strength Saving Throw: DC 17, one creature within 5 feet. Failure: The target is pushed up to 15 feet away from the stone golem and has the Prone condition.

Bonus Actions

Slow (Recharge 5-6). The stone golem casts the Slow spell, requiring no spell components and using Constitution as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17).

One Voice. When the drowned choir would be deafened or silenced, it sings through every throat it has drowned; a single failing voice does not stop the chord, and it is immune to being deafened.
Incorporeal Movement. The drowned choir can move through other creatures and objects as if they were difficult terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.
Sunlight Sensitivity. While in sunlight, the drowned choir has disadvantage on ability checks and attack rolls.
Ringing Chord (2/Day). As a bonus action, the drowned choir charges its next strike: the first time it hits with an attack before the end of its next turn, the target takes an extra 14 (4d6) necrotic damage.

Actions
Multiattack: The drowned choir makes two Mouths attacks.
Mouths: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 30 (6d8 + 3) necrotic damage.

```
Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

Speed: 

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

STR +9, CON +9

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Briar Regrowth. The vampire regains 20 hit points at the start of each of its turns if it has at least 1 hit point and thorns is within 30 feet of it.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair). If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., fly speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., fly speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks and uses Bite.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of two hands. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is pulled up to 10 feet straight toward the vampire.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A Humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a Vampire Spawn under the vampire's control.
```

AC 18
Initiative +5 (15)

Languages Common

CR 13 (XP 10,000; PB +5)

Traits

Brood Retort. A creature that touches the archon or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Poison damage.

Drifting. The archon doesn't provoke Opportunity Attacks when it flies out of an enemy's reach.

Drift Step. When a creature the archon can see ends its turn within 5 feet of it, the archon can move up to 10 feet without provoking Opportunity Attacks.

Actions

Multiattack. The archon makes three Talon attacks.

Talon. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 10 ft. 14 (2d8 + 5) Slashing damage plus 10 (3d6) Poison damage. If the archon flew at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 7 (2d6) Slashing damage and, if it is Large or smaller, has the Prone condition.

Stoop (Recharge 5-6). The archon flies up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. Each creature it passes within 5 feet during this movement is targeted once by the following effect. Strength Saving Throw: DC 17. Failure: 67 (15d8) Poison damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage.

Bonus Actions

Aerial Agility. The archon takes the Dash or Disengage action.

Broodcall (2/Day). Each ally within 30 feet of the archon gains 13 (3d8) Temporary Hit Points.

Purple Worm

Gargantuan Monstrosity, Unaligned

AC 18
Initiative -2 (8)

HP 247 (15d20 + 90)

Speed 50 ft., Burrow 50 ft.

Languages None

CR 15 (XP 13,000; PB +5)

Traits

Acid Burst. The first time the worm is Bloodied in a fight, it releases a burst. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the worm. Failure: 42 (12d6) Acid damage. Success: Half damage only.

Tunneler. The worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage. If the worm moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 22 (5d8) Acid damage and, if it is Huge or smaller, has the Prone condition. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage. If the worm moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 22 (5d8) Acid damage and, if it is Huge or smaller, has the Prone condition.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature Grappled by the worm (it can have up to three creatures swallowed at a time). Failure: The target is swallowed by the worm, and the Grappled condition ends. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the worm, and takes 17 (5d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the worm's turns.
If the worm takes 30 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, the worm must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 5 feet of the worm and has the Prone condition. If the worm dies, any swallowed creature no longer has the Restrained condition and can escape from the corpse using 20 feet of movement, exiting Prone.

[concept] lore: A war-band's supply column vanished on the deep road, and the purple worm that took the whole train of blades and mail still carries the armoury in its gut, surfacing wherever the smell of more iron — or more men — draws it.

Pit Fiend

Large Fiend, Lawful Evil

AC 21
Initiative +2 (12)

Speed 30 ft., Fly 60 ft.

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 20

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Oath-Fueled Detonation. The fiend explodes when it dies. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the fiend. Failure: 28 (8d6) Fire damage. Success: Half damage. The explosion destroys the fiend's body.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack. It can replace one attack with a use of Oath Reaping.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Fiery Mace. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (4d6 + 8) Force damage plus 21 (6d6) Fire damage.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Oath Reaping. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: 17 (5d6) Fire damage, and the fiend regains Hit Points equal to half the damage dealt.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

Bone Sun Lich
Medium undead, neutral evil
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

315 (42d8 + 126)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


11 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

16 (+3)

	

21 (+5)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Arcana +17, History +11, Insight +8, Perception +8

Saving Throws: 

CON +9, INT +11, WIS +8

Damage Resistances: 

cold, lightning

Damage Immunities: 

necrotic, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 18

Languages: 

All

Challenge

20 (25,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Shard Retort. A creature that touches the sun lich or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) necrotic damage.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the sun lich fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, the sun lich reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the sun lich's lair.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When the sun lich is hit by an attack roll, the sun lich casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: The sun lich makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Bone Rain or (B) Spellcasting to cast Ray of Sickness.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 19 Constitution saving throw or be paralyzed.
Spellcasting: The sun lich casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Bone Rain: One creature within 30 feet that is frightened of the sun lich must make a DC 19 Strength saving throw, taking 21 (6d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The sun lich can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The sun lich regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: The sun lich teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of the sun lich must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. The sun lich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: The sun lich casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. The sun lich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

[concept] identity: The Bone Sun Lich does not walk to battle — it arrives already seated, its throne bursting up through the floor as a spiraling corona of splintered femurs and ribs, and from that fixed height it rains grave-bolts and bone-withering blasts down on anything that tries to close. Each round it re-knits the sun's shattered bones into new facets and lance-formations, so the fight is a siege against a growing skeletal star rather than a duel, and it never leaves its throne willingly. Under real pressure it turns cold and theatrical, spending its crown-bones as ablative shields and holding its best spells to punish whatever broke through, fighting to the last shard with contemptuous patience.
[concept] lore: An archlich who could not bear to die in the dark forged its own sun of the dead, seating itself at its core so that it might rule a lightless realm from the only light it permits to shine.

Ancient Gold Dragon
Gargantuan dragon, lawful good
Armor Class

22 (Natural Armor)

Hit Points: 

546 (28d20 + 252)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


30 (+10)

	

14 (+2)

	

29 (+9)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

28 (+9)

Skills: 

Insight +10, Perception +17, Persuasion +16, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, CON +16, WIS +10, CHA +16

Damage Immunities: 

Fire

Senses: 

Blindsight 60 ft., Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 27

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

24

Proficiency Bonus:

+7

Amphibious. The dragon can breathe air and water.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon can use its Frightful Presence. It then makes three attacks: one with its bite and two with its claws.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (2d10 + 10) piercing damage.
Claw: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (2d6 + 10) slashing damage.
Tail: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 20 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) bludgeoning damage.
Frightful Presence: Each creature of the dragon's choice that is within 120 feet of the dragon and aware of it must succeed on a DC 24 Wisdom saving throw or become frightened for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. If a creature's saving throw is successful or the effect ends for it, the creature is immune to the dragon's Frightful Presence for the next 24 hours.
Breath Weapons (Recharge 5–6): The dragon uses one of the following breath weapons.
Fire Breath: The dragon exhales fire in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: The dragon exhales gas in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must succeed on a DC 24 Strength saving throw or have disadvantage on Strength-based attack rolls, Strength checks, and Strength saving throws for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.
Change Shape: The dragon magically polymorphs into a humanoid or beast that has a challenge rating no higher than its own, or back into its true form. It reverts to its true form if it dies. Any equipment it is wearing or carrying is absorbed or borne by the new form (the dragon's choice).

In a new form, the dragon retains its alignment, hit points, Hit Dice, ability to speak, proficiencies, Legendary Resistance, lair actions, and Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma scores, as well as this action. Its statistics and capabilities are otherwise replaced by those of the new form, except any class features or legendary actions of that form.
Legendary Actions

The ancient gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The ancient gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Detect: The dragon makes a Wisdom (Perception) check.
Tail Attack: The dragon makes a tail attack.
Wing Attack (Costs 2 Actions): The dragon beats its wings. Each creature within 15 feet of the dragon must succeed on a DC 25 Dexterity saving throw or take 17 (2d6 + 10) bludgeoning damage and be knocked prone. The dragon can then fly up to half its flying speed.
```

[concept] identity: The Ancient Gold Dragon opens by announcing itself — a molten roar that shakes the will of armies before its glowing golden hide even clears the clouds — then descends in sweeping passes, choosing between its searing breath and the crushing weight of claws and tail as the battlefield demands. Every round it takes it repositions to catch foes in a cone of fire, and it toys with prey from the air rather than committing to a single spot. When bloodied it does not flee but grows colder and more deliberate, using its fire to herd, corner, and finish rather than to open — a tyrant that fights to the death and considers restraint a personal insult.
[concept] lore: An Ancient Gold Dragon is a living horde that has outlived every empire it ever taxed, and it wants above all else for its hoard to keep growing — new treasures brought or burned from the world, either way.

[concept] identity: Vashka the Red plants his lacquered plate behind a locked shield wall and opens by advancing one slow, measured step until the front rank is in reach, daring the line to bend first. Every round after that he holds formation — shield high, blade working over the rim — punishing any foe who tries to slip past him with a hooked shield-bash and a short, brutal cut. He is a soldier, not a butcher: he never breaks ranks, never pursues a rout, and fights to the last with the cold patience of a commander who expects the line to hold.
[concept] lore: Vashka the Red rose from the rear ranks of a hobgoblin legion by never losing a shield wall, and now he sells that unbroken formation to whichever warlord pays — or crushes the one who doesn't.

Thresher of the Dead Hive

CR 9 (XP 5,000; PB +4)

Traits

Cracking Spray. The first time the thresher is Bloodied in a fight, it releases a burst. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature in a 15-foot Emanation originating from the thresher. Failure: 32 (5d12) Acid damage, and the target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Actions

Multiattack. The thresher makes two Scythe-Arm attacks.

Scythe-Arm. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 18 (4d6 + 4) Slashing damage plus 13 (3d8) Acid damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Caustic Spray (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 32 (5d12) Acid damage, and the target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Bonus Actions

Cage. The thresher cages one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While caged, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the thresher, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the thresher's turns. The thresher can have only one creature caged at a time. If the thresher takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the caged creature, the thresher must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the thresher. If the thresher dies, a caged creature pulls free Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

Iron Oath Sentinel
Huge construct, lawful neutral
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

168 (16d12 + 64)

Speed: 

20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

	

18 (+4)

	

7 (-2)

	

10 (+0)

	

3 (-4)

Skills: 

Perception +4

Saving Throws: 

CON +8

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 14

Languages: 

Common

Challenge

11 (7,200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Ringing Recoil. A creature that hits the oath sentinel with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) thunder damage and is deafened until the end of its next turn.
Ward Bond. The oath sentinel is magically bound to a ward of its choosing, and it can have only one ward at a time. While the oath sentinel is within 60 feet of its ward, half of any damage the ward takes (round up) is transferred to the oath sentinel.
Regeneration. The oath sentinel regains 10 hit points at the start of each of its turns if it has at least 1 hit point.
Interpose (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of the oath sentinel that it guards is hit by an attack roll, the attack hits the oath sentinel instead, and the oath sentinel has resistance to the attack's damage.
Oath Seam (Reaction). When a creature within 30 feet that the oath sentinel guards is hit by an attack roll, the oath sentinel moves up to half its speed toward the attacker without provoking opportunity attacks and makes one Iron Fist attack against it if it is within reach.

Actions
Multiattack: The oath sentinel makes two Iron Fist attacks.
Iron Fist: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (2d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 10 (3d6) force damage.
Warding Bulwark (2/Day): The oath sentinel targets itself or one creature it can see within 60 feet. The target gains 24 (7d6) temporary hit points, and it gains a +2 bonus to AC until the end of the oath sentinel's next turn.
```

[concept] identity: The sentinel wades into reach at a ponderous twenty feet and stays there, planting itself between its charge and the world with a body of riveted black iron that turns aside nearly every blow. Every round after the first it simply fights where it stands — hammering limbs of dense iron, bodying smaller foes aside, and refusing to yield ground it has claimed. It is bound, not enraged: it does not flee, does not pursue, does not tire, and will grind itself to slag in place before abandoning what it was made to guard.
[concept] lore: Forged in some dead smithy and bound by oath or geas to a single charge, the sentinel of black iron has stood unmoving in the dark for centuries, waiting for the one intruder foolish enough to test that vow.

AC 15
Initiative +2 (12)

Senses Passive Perception 14

Languages Common and any three languages

CR 7 (XP 2,900; PB +3)

Traits

Saltblast. The first time the yaga is Bloodied in a fight, it releases a burst. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 14, each creature in a 15-foot Emanation originating from the yaga. Failure: 17 (5d6) Bludgeoning damage, and the target has the Blinded condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Actions

Multiattack. The yaga makes three Arcane Burst attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Brine Bog Hex.

Arcane Burst. Melee or Ranged Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft. 16 (3d8 + 3) Force damage.

Spellcasting. The yaga casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 14):

- At Will: Detect Magic, Light, Mage Armor, Mage Hand, Prestidigitation
- 2/Day Each: Fireball, Invisibility
- 1/Day Each: Cone of Cold, Fly

Brine Bog Hex. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target's Speed is halved and it can't take Reactions until the end of the yaga's next turn.

Bonus Actions

Misty Step (3/Day). The yaga casts Misty Step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Reactions

Protective Magic (3/Day). The yaga casts Counterspell or Shield in response to the spell's trigger, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

[concept] identity: A flock of tarnished funeral bells drifts in silence until it finds the dying, then closes into a slow, clotted orbit overhead and begins to toll — a bone-deep knell that strips the last warmth from whoever lies beneath and stiffens them toward the grave. Each round it beats itself against the fading, ringing harder as the body fails, shaking loose a hail of chipped clappers and bronze shards at anything that tries to pull the victim free. It never lands and never flees; it is patient, greedy for last breaths, and will follow a wounded soul for miles rather than let another hand finish what it has begun.
[concept] lore: Born where a village buried its plague-dead in unconsecrated haste and their unmarked bells were left to molder, the ringers rise to claim the final toll that was denied them, gathering over any creature sliding toward death.

Every round after, it wheels and repositions to line up that same rolling breath before diving in to rake with claws and tail, always keeping its thunder corridor between itself and its prey. Elder Scalesheen
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

15

Hit Points: 

94 (21d8)

Speed: 

30 ft., climb 30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


10 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

passive Perception 15

+3

Hold Breath. The scalesheen can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost the scalesheen extra movement, and the scalesheen can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Patient Defense. As a bonus action, the scalesheen takes the Dodge action.
Cunning Action. As a bonus action, the scalesheen takes the Dash, Disengage, or Hide action.

Actions
Multiattack: The scalesheen makes two attacks: one with its Unarmed Strike and one with its Dart.
Unarmed Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 23 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. Once on each of the elder's turns when it hits with this attack, the target is subjected to the following effect. The target must succeed on a DC 14 Constitution saving throw or be stunned until the end of the elder's next turn.
Dart: Ranged Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) piercing damage.
Tail-Blow Drag (Recharge 5-6): Each creature within 10 feet of the scalesheen must make a DC 14 Dexterity saving throw, taking 17 (5d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
```

Wounded Frenzy. While it has half of its hit points or fewer, Meshrin has advantage on attack rolls and saving throws.
Hold Breath. Meshrin can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Great Weapon Master. When Meshrin hits a creature with its Greataxe, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost Meshrin extra movement, and Meshrin can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.

Actions
Multiattack: Meshrin makes three attacks, using Greataxe or Javelin. It can replace one attack with a use of Rattling War-Roar.
Greataxe: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) slashing damage.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, a javelin returns to Meshrin's grasp, and the target's speed is reduced by 10 feet until the start of Meshrin's next turn.
Rattling War-Roar: Each creature of Meshrin's choice within 30 feet of it that can see or hear it must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened of Meshrin until the end of its next turn.

Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (studded leather, shield)

112 (15d8 + 45)

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

Axefall Advance. While the sellsword is moving toward a creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, the sellsword's speed can't be reduced and difficult terrain doesn't cost it extra movement.
Riposte (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of the sellsword misses it with a melee attack roll, the sellsword makes one Handaxe attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: The sellsword makes three Handaxe attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Longbow.
Handaxe: Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d6 + 4) slashing damage, and the sellsword has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Longbow: The sellsword hurls a handaxe. Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 150/600 ft., one target. Hit: 9 (2d6 + 2) piercing damage. The weapon lands in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target; until the sellsword spends 5 feet of movement in that space to recover it, the sellsword's speed is halved and it can't use this action.
Shield Bash: One creature within 5 feet of the sellsword that the sellsword can see must succeed on a DC 15 Strength saving throw or take 9 (2d4 + 4) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it is prone.

[concept] identity: A disciplined sellsword who opens every fight at range, hurling a handaxe to stagger and bleed the quarry, then closes the distance with a shortsword while the axe arm recovers. Every round after that it circles, feinting a throw to keep prey at bay before driving in with short, economical cuts; it fights with a professional's patience and will break off bloodied rather than die for a contract. Its iron cap and bracers mark a mercenary who has survived long enough to know that the throw is a threat, not a finisher.
[concept] lore: A sellsword of the free companies, this fighter takes coin from whoever holds the local road and fights only as long as the pay (and blood) keeps its arithmetic favorable.

[concept] identity: Brother Halric opens by closing to melee behind his shield, hammer swinging, then settles into a grinding trade of blows where each hit he lands on himself or an ally is answered with a muttered battlefield prayer that mends flesh and hardens his guard. [concept] lore: Brother Halric was a crusading war-priest who fell on a battlefield no one came to claim, and he now walks the old campaign roads offering a last prayer to the dying and a hammer to anyone who won't kneel for it.
